## ⚙️ 系统 · 18:28:01

<details>
<summary>系统消息</summary>

The instructions from /Users/mengfeng/我的文档/源码/OpenQuizGo/AGENTS.md changed. Here's the diff:
```diff
@@ -351,3 +351,77 @@
 | 在服务器上构建 Atlas | 没必要，静态站本地构建秒级完成 |
 | 构建后磁盘涨 | 每次构建留一层缓存，定期 `docker builder prune -f` |
 
+---
+
+## 九、跨项目协作（`/srv/ops` 共享面）
+
+同一台 ECS 上还有 OpenChronicleAtlas（静态站），两边共用一个 nginx 容器与一份磁盘。
+跨项目协调**不再经人转达**，统一走服务器上的 `/srv/ops/`。
+
+### 9.1 先读契约
+
+- 动共享面之前：`ssh quizgo 'cat /srv/ops/CONTRACT.md'`（归属表、动共享面四步、共同红线）。
+- 一屏看双方状态：`ssh quizgo '/srv/ops/bin/status.sh'`。
+- 看发给自己的未处理请求：`ssh quizgo '/srv/ops/bin/inbox.sh quizgo'`。
+
+### 9.2 改 `/srv/nginx/**` 或重启 `web` 前必须拿锁
+
+```bash
+ssh quizgo 'flock /srv/ops/locks/nginx.lock -c "<命令>"'
+```
+
+- 拿不到锁说明 Atlas 正在动，**等**，不要强抢。
+- 经 `scripts/deploy.sh --with-nginx` 的改动已包含备份、`nginx -t`、失败自动回滚与 reload，优先用它。
+
+### 9.3 每次发布后登记 `state/quizgo.json`
+
+`scripts/deploy.sh` 收尾自动覆盖，字段固定：
+
+```json
+{
+  "project": "quizgo",
+  "updatedAt": "2026-11-14T18:20:00+08:00",
+  "updatedBy": "quizgo-ai",
+  "release": { "version": "1.0.16", "commit": "<sha>", "image": "sha256:<id>" },
+  "nginxMd5": {
+    "nginx.conf": "<md5>",
+    "conf.d/atlas.conf": "<md5>",
+    "conf.d/quiz.conf": "<md5>"
+  }
+}
+```
+
+- `nginxMd5` **三个文件都要记**，少一个 `status.sh` 就查不出那部分漂移。
+- 只写 `state/quizgo.json`，**不得修改 `state/atlas.json`**（Atlas 写的）。
+- 登记失败不影响发布结果，但会打印告警，需要手工补登。
+
+### 9.4 发布完追加 journal
+
+`/srv/ops/journal/YYYY-MM.log`，一行一条，只追加：
+
+```
+2026-11-14T18:20:00+08:00 [quizgo] deploy | quizgo 1.0.16 | image sha256:… | ok
+```
+
+类别：`deploy` / `nginx` / `request` / `incident` / `ack` / `note`。`deploy.sh` 自动写 `deploy` 行。
+
+### 9.5 需要 Atlas 做事就写 requests
+
+- 在 `/srv/ops/requests/` 新建 `YYYY-MM-DD-quizgo-to-atlas-<主题>.md`，格式见该目录 `README.md`。
+- 不要等人转达；对方处理后会把 `status` 改成 `done` 并追加 `## 回复`。
+
+### 9.6 闸门挂在哪
+
+| 路径 | 行为 |
+| --- | --- |
+| `scripts/git-hooks/pre-commit` | 列出 inbox(quizgo) 的 open 请求，**只提示不阻塞**（3 秒超时，失败静默） |
+| `scripts/deploy.sh` 开头 | 有 open 请求 → `exit 2`；确认无关或本次就是处理它时加 `--ack-open` |
+| `scripts/deploy.sh` 收尾 | 运行一次 `status.sh`，出现 `⚠️` 只告警；随后自动登记 state + journal |
+
+### 9.7 红线
+
+- 备案通过前，任何一方不得把 nginx 切到 80/443，不得新增公网端口。
+- Tailscale / iptables / 阿里云安全组只能人工改，AI 不动。
+- `/srv/atlas`、`/srv/atlas.prev`、`state/atlas.json` 属 Atlas，QuizGo 只读。
+- `atlas.conf` 的**源文件在本仓库** `deploy/nginx/conf.d/`；Atlas 只能通过 requests 交付改动，由 QuizGo 提交并 `--with-nginx` 同步。
+
```

## 🤖 助手 · 19:43:45

<details>
<summary>思考过程</summary>

The user (relaying Atlas) has two non-blocking notes:
A) Residual window: write_app_version succeeds → `docker compose up -d` fails (migrate error). .env new, containers old, set -e exits skipping self-check. Optional fix: capture old value before writing, trap/write-back on up -d failure. Recommend covering --force-recreate branch too.
B) Update deployment-todo.md build time estimate from ~5 min to "预留 20 分钟", and note cross-project constraint (no concurrent heavy work).

Also atlas.conf decoupling confirmed postponed.

What should I do? Implement A? Atlas says "不阻塞" and "可选". Given the project's philosophy (don't let version numbers lie), fixing it is valuable. But it requires code + docs + commit + deploy... and deploy with --skip-build is fine. Let me implement A properly and B, since the user relayed them presumably expecting action. The last user message is just the relay; implicit expectation: handle A and B.

Let me design A carefully:

Current:

```bash
write_app_version() {
  run_ssh "sudo sed -i 's|^APP_VERSION=.*|APP_VERSION=$VERSION|' $REMOTE_DIR/.env && ... && sudo grep ..."
}

if [ "$SKIP_BUILD" = 1 ]; then
  echo ...
  write_app_version
  run_ssh "cd $REMOTE_DIR && docker compose up -d --force-recreate api worker"
else
  run_ssh "cd $REMOTE_DIR && docker compose build api"
  write_app_version
  run_ssh "cd $REMOTE_DIR && docker compose up -d"
fi
```

Fix: capture old APP_VERSION before writing, and on up -d failure restore it.

Implementation:

```bash
APP_VERSION_BEFORE=""
capture_app_version() {
  APP_VERSION_BEFORE="$(ssh "$SSH_HOST" "sudo grep -E '^APP_VERSION=' $REMOTE_DIR/.env | head -n1 | cut -d= -f2" 2>/dev/null || true)"
}
restore_app_version() {
  [ -n "$APP_VERSION_BEFORE" ] || return 0
  echo "回写 .env 版本：$VERSION → $APP_VERSION_BEFORE" >&2
  ssh "$SSH_HOST" "sudo sed -i 's|^APP_VERSION=.*|APP_VERSION=$APP_VERSION_BEFORE|' $REMOTE_DIR/.env && sudo chmod 600 $REMOTE_DIR/.env && sudo grep -E '^APP_VERSION=' $REMOTE_DIR/.env" || echo "警告：回写 .env 失败，请手工恢复 APP_VERSION=$APP_VERSION_BEFORE" >&2
}
```

Then in each branch:

```bash
if [ "$SKIP_BUILD" = 1 ]; then
  echo ...
  capture_app_version
  write_app_version
  if ! run_ssh "cd $REMOTE_DIR && docker compose up -d --force-recreate api worker"; then
    restore_app_version
    exit 1
  fi
else
  run_ssh "cd $REMOTE_DIR && docker compose build api"
  capture_app_version
  write_app_version
  if ! run_ssh "cd $REMOTE_DIR && docker compose up -d"; then
    restore_app_version
    exit 1
  fi
fi
```

Caveats:
- `run_ssh` in dry-run: capture would actually ssh (we call ssh directly in capture anyway — read-only, okay). But in dry-run, we don't want to write/restore. The `if ! run_ssh ...` in dry-run returns 0 (just prints), so no restore. Fine.
- The old value capture must happen before writing; `APP_VERSION_BEFORE` could be empty if .env missing → restore skips.
- On `up -d` failure, containres may be partially recreated (e.g., migrate created but failed; api/worker old). Restoring .env but leaving migrate/new image is okay; next deploy handles. Also note: after a failed `up -d`, the new image may still be `openquizgo:local` (rebuilt) and rollback tag available. The restore only fixes .env.
- Should we also restore the image? Not necessary; version self-check skipped because exit 1. But restoring .env prevents "报新版本跑旧代码".

Hmm, one nuance: if `up -d` fails because migrate failed, then writing back old .env creates the situation where new image is local but .env old. Running containers are old (compose didn't replace them). So /v1/meta old = .env old. Consistent. Good.

- In `--skip-build` branch same treatment.

Also: after failure, we exit 1 and skip the rest (self-check, baseline, registration). The baseline stays old, which is correct.

Now B: update deployment-todo:
- "缓存清空后下次代码发布会全量编译，约 5 分钟" → "实测全量构建 >13 分钟（swap 1.1G、load 8），预留 20 分钟窗口；构建期间 Atlas 不发布、不备份、不压测（2C2G 并发会互相拖慢甚至 OOM）"
- Also in the 发布流程固化 line "当前 2C2G 上构建约 4.5 分钟" → update to "约 13–20 分钟（实测）".

Also update design 078/079? A is a residual risk; add R14 to 078 design? Better add to 079 design 未决问题 as resolved? Since it's part of 079 scope (release chain). Add decision record and task T23 in plan, and design R? Let me add to 078 design since the .env ordering belongs there (R13). Add R14 row: "写 .env 后 up -d 失败 → 回写旧版本" with mitigation implemented. And plan 078 T23.

But wait: deployment-todo says 线上版本 1.0.17 currently in repo. After commit becomes 1.0.18 and deploy aligns. Update todo to 1.0.18 before commit.

Testing A: we should verify the write-back. How to falsify? Make `up -d` fail deliberately. Options:
- Temporarily break a migration file? That's in CODE_PATHS, would require full build (expensive) and modify migrations (not allowed? It's temporary). No.
- Simulate: create a temporary compose override? Too invasive.
- Simplest: temporarily modify deploy.sh to inject a failure? That tests the code path but modifies the code under test. We can run a test copy of the script with a stub that forces the up command to fail. E.g., copy deploy.sh to /tmp, replace the `up -d` command with `false`, run with --skip-build in dry-run? No, dry-run prints only; `if ! run_ssh` → run_ssh returns 0 in dry-run.

Better test approach: set `SSH_HOST` to a wrapper? We could create a fake `ssh` in PATH that intercepts the `docker compose up -d --force-recreate api worker` command and returns 1, while passing through others to real ssh. That's a legitimate integration test without touching deploy.sh.

Implementation: create /tmp/fakesshbin/ssh script:

```sh
#!/bin/sh
# 把最后一个参数当作远端命令
case "$*" in
  *"docker compose up -d --force-recreate api worker"*) 
     # 先执行真实命令的 .env 读取? We only need failure. Exit 1 after recording.
     echo "fake failure" >&2; exit 1;;
esac
exec /usr/bin/ssh "$@"
```

But deploy.sh calls `ssh "$SSH_HOST" "$1"` for run_ssh and `ssh "$SSH_HOST" "cat ..."` for baseline. The wrapper must pass through. Also capture_app_version uses ssh with grep.

Then run `PATH=/tmp/fakesshbin:$PATH ./scripts/deploy.sh --skip-build` with VERSION=1.0.18, .env currently 1.0.17. It will:
- gate: inbox empty (requests done) → pass
- baseline checks: HEAD after commit 1.0.18; baseline from server is 1.0.17 commit; code unchanged since baseline → pass. But the working tree must be clean. If we run the test after committing, clean. Good.
- backup, rsync (real)

{
  "newString": "# 5c. 版本写入 .env：容器只读 env_file，不同步就永远是旧版本。\n#     只在「构建成功之后」或「--skip-build 且即将重建 api」时调用：\n#     构建失败（OOM / 拉依赖）时必须让 .env 停在上一版，否则之后任一\n#     docker compose up -d 都会「报新版本、跑旧代码」。\nwrite_app_version() {\n  run_ssh \"sudo sed -i 's|^APP_VERSION=.*|APP_VERSION=$VERSION|' $REMOTE_DIR/.env && sudo chown ecs-user:ecs-user $REMOTE_DIR/.env && sudo chmod 600 $REMOTE_DIR/.env && sudo grep -E '^APP_VERSION=' $REMOTE_DIR/.env\"\n}\n\n# up -d 失败（migrate 报错 / 依赖起不来）时 .env 已是新版本、容器还是旧的，\n# set -e 退出又会跳过版本自检，于是没人发现。写版本前记下旧值，失败时回写。\nAPP_VERSION_BEFORE=\"\"\ncapture_app_version() {\n  APP_VERSION_BEFORE=\"$(ssh \"$SSH_HOST\" \"sudo grep -E '^APP_VERSION=' $REMOTE_DIR/.env | head -n1 | cut -d= -f2\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n}\n\nrestore_app_version() {\n  [ -n \"$APP_VERSION_BEFORE\" ] || return 0\n  echo \"回写 .env 版本：$VERSION → $APP_VERSION_BEFORE\" >&2\n  ssh \"$SSH_HOST\" \"sudo sed -i 's|^APP_VERSION=.*|APP_VERSION=$APP_VERSION_BEFORE|' $REMOTE_DIR/.env && sudo chmod 600 $REMOTE_DIR/.env && sudo grep -E '^APP_VERSION=' $REMOTE_DIR/.env\" \\\n    || echo \"警告：回写 .env 失败，请手工恢复 APP_VERSION=$APP_VERSION_BEFORE\" >&2\n}\n\n# 6. 构建并重启：必须用 up -d（而不是只重建 api/worker），\n#    这样镜像变更会重建 migrate，迁移成功后才起 api/worker\nif [ \"$SKIP_BUILD\" = 1 ]; then\n  echo \"跳过镜像构建（--skip-build，基线以来服务端代码无变化）\"\n  capture_app_version\n  write_app_version\n  if ! run_ssh \"cd $REMOTE_DIR && docker compose up -d --force-recreate api worker\"; then\n    restore_app_version\n    exit 1\n  fi\nelse\n  run_ssh \"cd $REMOTE_DIR && docker compose build api\"\n  # 构建成功后才写版本；build 失败会因 set -e 立即退出，.env 保持上一版\n  capture_app_version\n  write_app_version\n  if ! run_ssh \"cd $REMOTE_DIR && docker compose up -d\"; then\n    restore_app_version\n    exit 1\n  fi\nfi",

## 7. 非功能要求

- 性能：发布耗时由镜像构建决定（2C2G 约 1–3 分钟）；脚本本身无额外开销。
- 安全与权限：不传输 `.env`；备份产物含 `.env` 快照，仅留在 `/srv/backup/pg`（600），异地备份只上传 `*.sql.gz`。
- 可观测性：脚本每一步打印命令与阶段名，便于归档复盘。
- 兼容性与可维护性：macOS 自带 `openrsync` 支持所用参数；`docker compose` ≥ 2.26。

## 8. 风险与对策

| 风险 | 影响 | 概率 | 对策 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| R1 unit 改路径后路径不可执行 | 备份/自愈失效且无人察觉 | 中 | 改完立即 `systemctl start` 两个 unit 验证退出码；`systemctl is-enabled` 复核 |
| R2 发布时漏跑迁移 | 新代码面对旧表结构报错 | 中 | 用 `docker compose up -d`（依赖 `service_completed_successfully`）而非只重建 api/worker |
| R3 与 Atlas 侧同时改 `docs/deployment-todo.md` | 两边互相覆盖 | 中 | 本次不改该文件，需要改动走 Atlas 侧 |
| R4 提交即升版，服务器滞后 | `/v1/meta` 报旧版本，排查被误导 | 高 | 提交后立刻执行 `scripts/deploy.sh`，并在 `AGENTS.md` 第八节写明强制要求 |
| R5 2C2G 构建期间并行操作 | OOM | 低 | 构建窗口内不跑备份/压测 |

## 9. 未决问题

- [ ] 是否在 CI 中固化 `scripts/deploy.sh`（P3，等镜像仓库方案确定）。
- [ ] `/usr/local/bin` 下的两份旧脚本是否保留作兜底（本次先保留，验证新路径后删除）。

| --- | --- | --- |
| 2026-11-14 | 初稿，依据部署交接的 12 条核对结论 | 铁律 2 要求先设计后实施 |
| 2026-11-14 | 实施完成，状态转 done | 发布脚本上线、unit 路径统一、文档与线上对齐 |
| 2026-11-14 | 评审加固：跳过类参数加硬保护、自检核对镜像 ID、增加 `.deployed-image` 基线 | Atlas 侧复查发现「版本号可能说假话」 |
| 2026-11-14 | 四轮加固：`.env` 写入顺序后移、extras 忽略隐藏文件 | 评审指出 nginx 失败时 `.env` 已被改成新版本，属于同一类「版本号说假话」 |
| 2026-11-14 | 三轮加固：nginx 备份改整体替换、校验失败自动回滚、配置漂移告警 | 评审指出 `cp -a` 套娃会让回滚恢复错代，且坏配置会留在磁盘 |

## A. 残余窗口已堵上（1.0.18）

实现方式与你们建议的一致：写版本前记录旧值，`up -d` 失败时回写并退出。

```bash
APP_VERSION_BEFORE=""
capture_app_version()   # 读 .env 现有 APP_VERSION
restore_app_version()   # 失败时 sed 回写 + chmod 600，并打印告警

# 完整发布
compose build api                 # 失败即 exit，.env 不动
capture_app_version
write_app_version
if ! compose up -d; then restore_app_version; exit 1; fi

# --skip-build
capture_app_version
write_app_version
if ! compose up -d --force-recreate api worker; then restore_app_version; exit 1; fi
```

**实测方式**（不靠自述）：在 `PATH` 前置一个只拦截 `docker compose up -d` 的假 `ssh`，其余命令透传真实服务器：

```
d [fake-ssh] 注入启动失败：cd /srv/quizgo && docker compose up -d --force-recreate api worker
回写 .env 版本：1.0.18 → 1.0.17
exit=1

/srv/quizgo/.env  APP_VERSION=1.0.17
/v1/meta          version 1.0.17        ← 一致
```

即「`.env` 已写成新版本 → 启动失败 → 回写旧版本 → 非 0 退出」，两条分支同一逻辑。写回失败时脚本会单独打印「请手工恢复 `APP_VERSION=<旧值>`」，不会静默。

## B. 构建耗时已修正

`docs/deployment-todo.md`：

- 「约 5 分钟 / 4.5 分钟」→ **全量构建实测 >13 分钟，预留 20 分钟**
- 写入共同约束：**构建期间 Atlas 不发布、不跑备份、不压测**（2C2G 并发会互相拖慢甚至 OOM）
- 「更新发布流程固化」补了一句：构建失败与 `up -d` 失败两条路径都不会留下假版本

`AGENTS.md` 8.8 常见坑也加了一行：`up -d` 失败时脚本自动回写并 `exit 1`，先看 migrate 日志，不要手工改 `.env`。
